# M0 gate benchmark record — edit-zzj.5

Baseline: `c0ae480f06ba409f730cb425facb753f4cab67cf` (deep-file typing fix included). Measurement-only campaign; source, benches and tests are unchanged.

Runs use the loaded laptop as it is, without quiet-box waits. All display-dependent rows use **Xvfb :99, synthetic MSC**. G2c/optical latency and actual displayed-refresh G3z cannot be proven here. A printed PASS for a component or an undersampled legacy harness is retained as that harness verdict, not promoted to complete acceptance.

Each binary is invoked individually with `timeout 600`; existing `.args` files are consumed verbatim. The priority sequence overlaps the long scroll/save sequence, and sanitizer build/check/fuzz work overlaps portions of this loaded campaign; these are not isolated timing conditions. Additional section runs recover rows skipped by an earlier process failure. No timing is used as a tuning decision. The within-20% flag is a derived comparison: the largest constrained latency/gate ratio (smallest throughput/gate ratio for throughput) lies from 0.8 through 1.2. A miss within this band is eligible for policy rerun; no repeat seeks a quieter box. A zero percentile limit means that percentile is not gated. Confidence intervals and sample counts are preserved in the raw output. Missing CI fields are **not printed by the existing bench**, not inferred.

## Environment

- Commit: `c0ae480f06ba409f730cb425facb753f4cab67cf edit-czn: editor: typing deep in an unindexed 1 GiB file no longer replays the prefix on the UI thread (layout uses bounded/estimated line starts); G1 row at 0.9 x lines completes (tails still over gate on a loaded box)`.
- BAT0 before/after every run: Not charging (M)[AC].
- Stamped one-minute load range: 1.84–11.36 (M)[AC]; all three uptime averages and exact timestamps follow per run.
- Display: `DISPLAY=:99 EDIT_DISPLAY=:99`; Xvfb, 2880×1800, synthetic MSC. No :0 invocation.
- Release compiler: `gcc (Ubuntu 13.3.0-6ubuntu2~24.04.1) 13.3.0`; sanitizer compiler: `Ubuntu clang version 18.1.3 (1ubuntu1)`.
- Kernel: `Linux 6.8.0-146-generic`.
- Release build: `DISPLAY=:99 EDIT_DISPLAY=:99 make all`, exit 0; `-std=c11 -Wall -Wextra -Werror -Wshadow -Wconversion`, `-O2 -g -msse2`.
- Existing corpus fixtures are read under `/tmp/edit-corpus`; no regeneration. `log_1g.txt` has 8,947,842 lines (provided fixture metadata); `sparse_10g.bin` is the sparse fixture; `all_a_1g.txt` is available.
- Runtime and TMPDIR override: `$REPO/build/m0/runtime`; `EDIT_ALLOW_REAL_DISPLAY` and `EDIT_BACKEND` unset for default runs.
